**Fixtures.** `fixtures/ext/`: `registration.template.yaml` and `spec.template.yaml` (rendered by `_ext.ExtScenario`; the clause, seeds and bootstrap equal the Tier B fixture's), `trainer.yaml`, `launch-request.golden.yaml` with `launch-command.golden.txt` (the hashed label written `<label>` and the `/data/...` paths relative to a test root, EXT-46, EXT-51; area1-trl README shape, EXT-24), `venv.lock.fixture` (the golden request's `venv_lock`, copied into the working directory as `uv.lock` by `test_ext_launch.launch`), `gpu-log.fixture.md` (running total 3.12), `gpu-guard.fixture.log` (gpu-step.sh format; label `ext-synthetic-full`, formerly `ext-a1-trl-full`, sums to 3902 s). Built on the fly by `_ext` (since the move: for the synthetic adapter, the training script `train.py`, framework `synthetic-trainer`, `run_args.json` with `rows_per_step` 4, runtime stand-in `synthetic-runtime`; the names below are those of the TRL fixture as written): GSM8K item set and training shards from `fixtures/tierb/gsm8k-source` (30 test items, 40 training rows, disjoint), an HF base directory, the export layout `<root>/export/{train/, train.jsonl}` (the spec's training shards are `export/train`, EXT-65), the host layout (host work `<root>/host-work` with `gpu-step.sh`, `gpu-guard.log`, `gpu-log.md`; output root `<root>/out`; input root `<root>`; `ExtScenario.env()`, passed to `run_cli(..., env=)`; `test_ext_launch.host_layout` for the golden request: host work `/data/gatedtrain-work`, output root `/data/gatedtrain-mm/out`, input root `/data`), the training-script stand-in `<root>/code/train_grpo.py` (mounted read-only at `/code`, the record's `script_path` `/code/train_grpo.py` and `script_sha256`, EXT-83; the stage output's `env-runtime.json` carries the same `script_sha256`), a TRL output directory at `<root>/out/a1/stage-out` as `train_grpo.py` leaves it (`checkpoint-5`, `checkpoint-10` in the transformers 5.17.0 cut layout with no `_name_or_path`, the model card `README.md`, `env-freeze.txt`, `env-runtime.json`, `run_args.json` for an unshuffled run of 4 prompts per step, `samples.jsonl`, `memory.jsonl`, `attempts.jsonl`; `_ext.shuffle_args` makes a shuffled run) with valid header-only safetensors stubs, two arms, launch and smoke records (the launch label hashed over the record, `_ext.launch_label`, EXT-46), a GPU log (2400 s) of that label, and a `<host_work>/bin/gpu-step.sh` stand-in with the `WORK=` and `LOG=` lines ext-collect reads (never executed). Review round 4: the fixture launch record mounts the code directory at `/code` (so the appended mount in collect tests is `mounts[4]`), records `allow_network: false`, `script_path`, `script_sha256` and `host_config_sha256` (`_ext.host_config_sha256` over the scenario's layout); `test_ext_launch.launch` passes `--spec` written by `_ext.write_bound_spec` for the golden request's base and export (`bound_spec`; `spec=False` omits it, EXT-84), and `rooted` creates a stub for a moved `.py` argv element (the golden `train_grpo.py`, EXT-83). Review round 5: `rooted` moves host paths only (mount sources, host_work, output_dir; container paths and argv stay `/data/...`, since under the test root they would lie inside the `/tmp` tmpfs, EXT-94) and creates the interpreter stub `<root>/data/gatedtrain-mm/area1-trl/.venv/bin/python` (EXT-91); the golden request drops `network_host_reason` (EXT-97); the fixture launch record is `schema_version` 2.0, runs `/code/bin/python3` (a stub in the code directory, `interpreter_path`, `interpreter_sha256`) and has no `network_host_reason`; `test_ext_review2.unit_config` carries the TRL environment allow-list.

**Design choices.** (1) Item not in the set refuses (EXT-1), against MM-6's MISSING. (2) Evaluator errors are gate-missing items, not exit 3 (EXT-4). (3) Model-hash mismatch is MISSING (EXT-11). (4) Evaluator identity hash fields (EXT-6). (5) Component list (EXT-14) and top-level final model not a cut (EXT-13). (6) `run_args.json` `model_name_or_path` absent refuses (EXT-20; was `config.json` `_name_or_path`, which transformers 5.17.0 never writes). (7) Impact `first_step` and `derivation` null for `stage` edges (EXT-19). (8) Binary metrics skip the smoke mode rule; the 5 % boundary is allowed (EXT-29). (9) A START without a terminator refuses (EXT-28). (10) `training_seed` from the launch record (EXT-32), which must equal `run_args.json` `seed` (EXT-50). (11) `records/` copies of the launch and smoke records (EXT-2). (12) Adapter name `trl_grpo` (MM §6), not `trl`. (13) New registration key `contamination.allow_exact_overlap` (EXT-21). (14) The trainer config shape `{backend, profile}` (EXT-2).

**Design gaps.** EXT-G1 (derivation of a `stage` answer) and EXT-G2 (cross-run closure through `used` model edges, coarsest precision on the path) are resolved by design revision 13, R13-10 (05 §11.3). EXT-G3 (binary metrics and the smoke degeneracy rules) is resolved by maintainers' decision in MM-38: binary metrics skip the 5 % and the 50 % rules and are degenerate only at mean 0 or 1. EXT-G4 (no producer of `eval_meta.json`) is resolved in this slice by `ext-from-nel` (EXT-35 to EXT-38). EXT-G5 (MM-2 types `item` as a string, 05 §3.8 allows any hashable ID): a prototype choice, recorded as a prototype gap: item IDs are JSON strings only; no design change.

**Deferred (later slices).** Observed sample logs (MM-26, MM-27), exposure (MM-28); LoRA identity and `lora_compose` (MM-18), embodied normalizer and env version (MM-19); two-stage chains with mixed precision (MM-15; area 6); the WER and T0 report fixtures (MM-8, MM-16; areas 5 and 2a); constraint clauses through `ext-collect` (MM-C1 to MM-C4, first generative area); the record-level deviation for root images (MM-34); registry rows of areas 2a to 8; transitive impact across tiers (Tier A and Tier B lineage carries no `model_hash`, so a run starting from a Tier A/B checkpoint is not reached through it; `docs/findings.md`).

**State before implementation (2026-11-11, after the maintainers' decisions).** 120 tests (counting each test method once; `test_ext_from_nel.py` 10 of them). 4 pass as regressions: `TestSpecIds.test_unregistered_trainer_refused`, `test_unregistered_evaluator_refused`, `test_run_refuses_ext_spec`, `TestLaunchRules.test_credential_value_never_printed` (vacuous until `ext-launch` exists). The others fail for the intended reasons: `ModuleNotFoundError: gatedtrain.ext` (registry, adapter, `gpu_seconds`, `smoke_verdict`); exit 2 from argparse for the unknown commands `ext-collect`, `ext-merge-eval`, `ext-launch`, `ext-from-nel` (in the round trip, `merge-eval` and `tierb-collect` already pass on the same runs); `validate` refusing `rl.trl_grpo@0.1` (exit 3, `SPEC-REF`); `tierb-collect` refusing the ext spec at validation (`SPEC-REF`, `REG-FIELD`) instead of `TIERB-SPEC`.

### 11.19 External-run adapters, slice 2: area "control" (SB3 PPO hover) (2026-11-12)

**Moved.** The adapter `sb3_ppo`, its registry rows and the tests below are in `gatedtrain-recipe-sb3-ppo-drone-hover` (section 11.28); file names below are those of the time of writing.

**Sources.** `MULTIMODAL-SPEC.md` section 6 row 4 with its accepted deviations (bounded deciding metric, N = 300, base cut 8), MM-19, MM-21, MM-25, MM-27, MM-31, MM-36, MM-38; the area runtime `<mm-root>/area2-drones/README.md` ("What gatedtrain needs", "Outputs of `train_ppo.py`", "Mounts and command shape", read 2026-11-12) and its sanity outputs; design revision 14: 05 §11.2 (derivation, R11-18, C4), §11.3 (closure across runs through `used` model edges), §12.1 (environment parameters as a `DatasetSnapshot`, R11-17, C3), §12.2 (an `iteration` record has null `row`, `chunk`, `seed_inputs`), §3.3 and §3.7 (an item is an episode seed with environment parameters; `eval_distribution.env_version`, R11-19, C5).

**Interfaces.** `gatedtrain/ext/adapters/sb3_ppo.py` (`Sb3PpoAdapter`: the MM-12 interface plus `normalizer_sha256`, `env_version_sha256`, `consumption_problems`, `training_payloads`, `uses_env`); registry rows `sb3_ppo`, `drones.sb3_items@0.1`, `drones.sb3_return_items@0.1`; `gatedtrain/ext/fill.py` and `gatedtrain ext-arm-fill --adapter A --checkpoint CUT --items DIR ARM`. Generic hooks in `ext/collect.py` (used only when an adapter or evaluator row declares them), `ext/launch.py` (CPU steps, own output as input), `registration.py` (evaluator-declared sampler keys), `report.py` (`wall_seconds`).

#### Requirements

| ID | Source | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| EXT-98 | MM-37, §6 row 4 and deviations | **Registry rows.** Trainer `sb3_ppo` (`rl.sb3_ppo@0.1`; pins stable-baselines3, gymnasium, gym-pybullet-drones, pybullet; components `policy.pth`, `policy_meta.json`, `vecnormalize.pkl`, `vecnormalize.json`, `env_version.json`; consumption `iteration`, no fallback; evaluators deciding first). Evaluators `drones.sb3_items@0.1` (`hover_success`, T3) and `drones.sb3_return_items@0.1` (`hover_score`, T0): higher, [0, 1], not binary, error class `simulator_error`, score outcomes crash, below_tolerance, timeout, payload text field `payload`, item payload `init` (a JSON object, `item_payload_objects`) and `payload`, `normalizer` and `env_version` true, scorer code SHA-256 = `eval_hover.py`'s. The docs tables (§6 rows 4 and 4s) match. |
| EXT-99 | MM-17, MM-19, MM-20 | **Cuts.** `checkpoint-<I>/` is a cut iff `cut.json` parses with `iteration` I, lists exactly the six other files with their SHA-256 (all match), the directory holds nothing else, and `model.zip`'s `policy.pth` member (stdlib `zipfile`, bytes only) is the cut's `policy.pth`. `.checkpoint-<I>.partial/` is never a cut. `model_hash` covers the five components; `cut_hash` the whole directory. `vecnormalize.pkl` is hashed, never opened. |
| EXT-100 | MM-34, EXT-79, EXT-92, EXT-93 | **Host rows.** Input roots: the Python directory, `area2-drones`, and `out/area2-drones` (the base runs' outputs); env names `HF_HUB_OFFLINE`, `HOME`, `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE`, `PYTHONUNBUFFERED`, `XDG_CACHE_HOME`; scan skip `area2-drones/.venv` and the interpreter's site-packages; interpreter tree CPython 3.12.16. `spec_binding` false: the base phase has no spec yet, `--spec` is optional and checked when given. |
| EXT-101 | MM-15, MM-21, 05 §11.3 | **Two phases, base binding** (`EXT-BASE artifacts.<base>.uri`). The spec's base is a cut of a base-phase run; ext-collect gates the continue-phase run: `run_args.json` `phase: continue`, `base_checkpoint` = the container path of the read-only mount whose host path is the spec's base, `base_cut` = that cut's `cut.json` (`files` and `iteration`), `first_iteration` = base iteration + 1; the base cut's parent holds the base run, `phase: base`, `base_checkpoint: null` (longer chains refused). The training stage `used` the base cut's cut hash and model hash; the baseline arm must be that model hash. |
| EXT-102 | MM-25, MM-27, 05 §11.2, §12.2, §12.4 | **Observed consumption** (`EXT-CONSUMPTION --stage-out`, before writing). `rollouts.jsonl` covers `first_iteration` .. the last cut, one line each, in order; each chunk is `n_steps × n_envs` env steps, contiguous with the previous; its episodes' steps sum to that; its shard is the spec's one env-params shard. Later lines are allowed (orphaned). Ledger: one row per (iteration, shard) of this run only (never the base run's), `precision: iteration`, `derivation: observed`, `data_ref.snapshot` = manifest of the spec's shard directory, `row`, `chunk`, `seed_inputs` null. The impact query on the shard returns the continuation's cuts (first step = `first_iteration`); the base cut is a `used` input and is not returned from this run directory. |
| EXT-103 | MM-19, MM-21 (EXT-65 analogue), C3, C5 | **Data and environment binding.** `run_args.json` `env_params_file` resolves through the read-only mounts (`launch.container_file`) to the spec's shard and `env_params_sha256` is its SHA-256 (`EXT-DATA`); with a registered `eval_distribution.env_version`, `run_args.json` `env_version_sha256`, the last cut's and the base cut's `env_version.json` equal it, and `env_params_version` equals the registered one (`EXT-ENV`). An environment evaluator with no registered `env_version` refuses (`REG-FIELD registration.eval_distribution.env_version`). |
| EXT-104 | MM-34, EXT-63 | **Input root under the output root.** A read-only mount at or inside the run's own output directory (lexical or resolved) refuses (`EXT-LAUNCH ...mounts[i].host`); a parent of the output still refuses; another run's cut under the output root is accepted as an input. |
| EXT-105 | MM-31, MM-36, MM-38 | **CPU accounting.** `gpus: false`: `gpu_est_h >= 0` (0 allowed; negative refuses), a full run's smoke projection `>= 0`; `gpus: true` unchanged (`> 0`). ext-collect: `gpu_seconds` 0 for `gpus: false`, the START/END sum otherwise (as before); lockfile and report add `wall_seconds` (the START/END sum) for every run. A full run's smoke record is judged on the adapter's first (deciding) evaluator. |
| EXT-106 | MM-2, 05 §3.3 | **Policy sampler.** A deciding evaluator whose registry row declares `sampler_keys` registers exactly those sampler keys (`policy`, `vecnormalize`, `episodes_per_item`, `max_steps` for the drones rows) and none of the LLM rules apply; other evaluators keep the LLM rules. |
| EXT-107 | MM-11 | **Exact overlap against training episodes.** For `sb3_ppo` the item `payload` strings are compared with every `episodes[].payload` of the base run's `rollouts.jsonl` up to the base cut and this run's up to its last cut, not with the shard text; a log that cannot be read refuses (`EXT-CONTAMINATION --stage-out`). |
| EXT-108 | MM-29, MM-33, EXT-50, EXT-68 | **Seed, pins, profile, last cut.** `run_args.json` `seed` = `training_seed` (`EXT-LAUNCH`); `env-freeze.txt` has `stable-baselines3==<launch framework version>` and `# gym-pybullet-drones commit <40 hex>` (`EXT-FREEZE`); `profile` = the record's (`EXT-PROFILE`); the last cut's iteration = `last_iteration` (`EXT-STAGE`). |
| EXT-109 | MM-19 | **Arm normalizer and environment** (`EXT-ARM`). With `normalizer` in the evaluator row, each arm's `normalizer_sha256` is its evaluated cut's `vecnormalize.pkl` SHA-256 (baseline: the base cut; candidate: the last cut); with `env_version`, `env_version_sha256` is the registered one. Area 1 (both false) still requires null. |
| EXT-110 | MM-1, MM-2, MM-17 | **`ext-arm-fill`.** Computes `checkpoint.model_hash` (adapter identity of `--checkpoint`, a complete cut), `item_set_hash` (manifest of `--items`) and `sampler_hash` (RFC 8785 SHA-256 of the arm's `sampler`); writes the null ones (canonical JSON, other keys kept), verifies the set ones; refuses (`EXT-FILL`, nothing written) a mismatch, an arm `normalizer_sha256` or `env_version_sha256` that is not the cut's, an incomplete cut, or an arm of another adapter or evaluator. Never touches `items.jsonl`. |
| EXT-111 | MM-9 to MM-11 | **Whole continuation collected:** PASS on the fixture, baseline and candidate model hashes, contamination 0, precision `iteration`, 300 items. |
| EXT-112 | MM-34, review round 6 Medium-1 | **Container paths** (`EXT-LAUNCH ...mounts[i].container`, `...tmpfs`; request and record, both adapters). Registry `container_paths` per adapter: a read-only mount at its own host path (lexical or resolved) inside an input root, or strictly under `/inputs/`; the read-write mount at `/out`; a tmpfs at `/tmp` (normal form). Whatever the row says, no mount or tmpfs at `/` or at or under `/etc`, `/usr`, `/bin`, `/sbin`, `/lib*`, `/opt`, `/proc`, `/sys`, `/dev`, `/run`, `/var`, `/root`, `/home`, except a read-only identity mount of an input root. Without an adapter row every container path refuses. `GATEDTRAIN_EXT_MIRROR_ROOT` (tests) is the host directory that stands for `/` in an identity mount. The reviewer's `/etc/ld.so.preload`, `/opt/nvidia/nvidia_entrypoint.sh`, `/usr/lib/python3` refuse. |
| EXT-113 | MM-34, review round 6 Low-1 | **Pinned interpreter** (`...argv`). argv[0] equals the registry's `interpreter` string exactly (TRL `<mm-root>/area1-trl/.venv/bin/python`, SB3 `.../area2-drones/.venv/bin/python`; unresolved, since CPython finds the venv from it) and the host file it resolves to lies inside one of the adapter's `interpreter_trees`; at ext-launch, `--recheck` and ext-collect. Overrides `GATEDTRAIN_EXT_INTERPRETER`, `GATEDTRAIN_EXT_INTERPRETER_TREES` (tests). |
| EXT-114 | MM-34, MM-35, review round 6 Low-2 | **Path-valued environment values** (`...env.<NAME>`, request only: the record keeps names). Registry `env_paths` (TRL: `FLASHINFER_WORKSPACE_BASE`, `HF_HOME`, `HOME`, `TORCHINDUCTOR_CACHE_DIR`, `TRITON_CACHE_DIR`, `VLLM_CACHE_ROOT`, `VLLM_CONFIG_ROOT`, `XDG_CACHE_HOME`; SB3: `HOME`, `XDG_CACHE_HOME`): the value is absolute and its normal form is at or under `/tmp` or `/out`; the value is never shown. |
| EXT-115 | MM-34, review round 6 Low-3 | **Each tmpfs path once** (`...tmpfs`): duplicates in normal form (`/tmp`, `/tmp/`, `//tmp`, `/tmp/.`) refuse. |
| EXT-117 | MM-35, EXT-85 (maintainers' decision, round 7 Q1) | **Overrides hashed.** `host_config_sha256` covers `mirror_root`, `interpreter` and `interpreter_trees` of the effective layout (after the adapter's rows are filled) besides host work, output root and input roots, so a record written under `GATEDTRAIN_EXT_MIRROR_ROOT`, `GATEDTRAIN_EXT_INTERPRETER` or `GATEDTRAIN_EXT_INTERPRETER_TREES` refuses (`...host_config_sha256`) at ext-collect and `--recheck` under another value, also where every other rule passes (another tree that still holds the interpreter). |
| EXT-116 | review round 6 mutants N2, N4, N9 | **Tests only:** a 40-link chain binds and a 41-link chain refuses; `s.py -> evil.sh` refuses as not a `.py` script; `<tree>/lib/python3.12/x/secrets.cpython-312.pyc` refuses while the `__pycache__` one is exempt. |

**Fixtures.** `fixtures/ext/sb3/`: `env_version.json` and `hover-init-v1.jsonl` (byte copies of `area2-drones/env-snapshot/`), `policy_meta.json` and `env-freeze.txt` (from the 2026-11-12 continue sanity run from base cut 8), `spec.template.yaml` and `registration.template.yaml` (the area's gate shape: `hover_success`, superiority, margin 0, sigma 0.39, alpha 0.05, power 0.8, planning effect 0.06, N 300, K 1, eval seed 0, `eval_distribution.env_version` and `env_params_version`, the policy sampler). Built by `_sb3.Sb3Scenario`: a runtime stand-in mounted at `/a2`, 300 items, a base run (iterations 1-2, cuts 1 and 2; cut 2 is the spec's base) and a continuation (iterations 3-6, cuts 4 and 6) with 2 envs × 8 steps per rollout, two `eval_hover.py`-shaped arms, a CPU launch record (`gpus: false`, `gpu_est_h: 0`), a smoke record, and a 105 s guard log. Weights and the pickled normalizer are byte stand-ins.

**Design choices.** (1) `iteration` ledger rows carry null `chunk` and `seed_inputs` (05 §12.2); MM-25 now says the same (review round 7; it used to name a chunk and seed). (2) The base run's consumption is not in the continuation's ledger; it is reachable only through a run directory of its own (none exists: `ext-collect` needs a gate). (3) `--spec` optional for `sb3_ppo`. (4) A CPU step may carry `gpu_est_h > 0` (accepted, logged 0). (5) `wall_seconds` added for every adapter (additive key; area 1's `gpu_seconds` unchanged). (6) The smoke record of a multi-evaluator adapter is judged on its first evaluator. (7) The spec's training data is the shard directory `env-snapshot/shards/`, so `data_ref.snapshot` is its manifest (`sha256:d12f691e...`), not the whole snapshot's (`sha256:f4e0fa91...`) that the area README names.

**State before implementation (2026-11-12).** 60 tests at the time (2 added later with mutants M51 and M52): against a copy of the tree before this slice, 57 failed (70 failing sub-results) and 3 passed as regressions (`test_scorer_code_hash_is_eval_hover`, `test_llm_rules_unchanged_for_area_1`, `test_trl_arms_still_null`). Existing tests unchanged; `test_ext_*.py` now 474.

**Operational choices, area 2 (maintainers, 2026-11-12; recorded in review round 7, no code).** (1) The base phase is launched with `ext-launch --profile smoke` while its script arguments stay `--phase base --profile full`: a full-profile launch needs a PASS smoke record, which needs an evaluated base cut that only the base phase produces. The base run is never collected (`ext-collect` refuses a base-phase run as a candidate, EXT-101). (2) Known gap, disclosed: impact queries do not return the base phase's cuts (the base run's consumption enters no ledger; open, choice (2) above). (3) `gpu-step.sh` counts CPU wall time against the cap: its budget check adds the wall time of the label's earlier attempts to `GATEDTRAIN_GPU_USED_H + GATEDTRAIN_GPU_STEP_EST_H` and does not know the step is CPU-only; conservative (a retry may be refused early, never admitted late), accepted; `ext-collect` still logs 0 GPU-seconds (EXT-105).

**Review round 7 (EXT-112 to EXT-117; tests in `tests/test_ext_review6.py`, 42).** EXT-117 (maintainers' question Q1, after the first fix): its 5 tests failed before `host_config_sha256` took the three values (`test_same_layout_passes` passed as a regression check); `_ext.host_config_sha256` takes `mirror_root`, `interpreter`, `interpreter_trees` and the `ExtScenario`, `Sb3Scenario` records and `test_ext_review4...test_recorded_and_warned` pass them. Before the change 31 of the 35 tests then written failed (unittest: 19 failures, 173 errors, counting sub-tests); 4 passed: the three EXT-116 tests (they target mutants) and `TestContainerPathsLaunch.test_golden_accepted` (a regression check). Two tests were added after the mutant run (R10, R12, R20). Test helpers changed for the new rules (intended): `_ext.CODE_CONTAINER` `/code` -> `/inputs/code`, `_ext.BASE_CONTAINER` `/models/base-model` -> `/inputs/base-model`, `_sb3.RUNTIME_CONTAINER` `/a2` -> `/inputs/a2`; `ExtScenario.env`, `Sb3Scenario.env`, `test_ext_launch.host_layout` and the `--recheck` helpers of `test_ext_review3`/`test_ext_review4` set `GATEDTRAIN_EXT_MIRROR_ROOT` (golden only), `GATEDTRAIN_EXT_INTERPRETER` and `GATEDTRAIN_EXT_INTERPRETER_TREES`; `test_ext_review2.unit_config` adds the TRL container paths, the mirror root and `env_paths`. Assertions changed: `test_ext_sb3.LaunchSb3.request` sets `HOME=/tmp/home`, `XDG_CACHE_HOME=/tmp/cache` (was `1`); `test_ext_review4.TestShapes.test_accepted` puts the whitespace value in `MASTER_ADDR` (was `HOME`) and drops the `/scratch` tmpfs; `test_ext_review5.TestNoNesting.test_siblings_accepted` drops the `/scratch` tmpfs; `test_ext_launch.TestLaunchRules.test_venv_in_container_records_lock_hash` mounts the venv at `/inputs/venv` (was `/venv`); `test_ext_collect...test_base_not_mounted` uses `BASE_CONTAINER`; `test_ext_review3.scenario_request` uses `SCRIPT_CONTAINER`.

### 11.20 External-run adapters, slice 3: area "image generation, SD1.5" (DDPO + LoRA) (2026-11-12)

**Moved.** The adapter `ddpo_sd15`, its registry rows and the tests below are in `gatedtrain-recipe-ddpo-sd15` (section 11.28); file names below are those of the time of writing.

**Sources.** `MULTIMODAL-SPEC.md` section 6 row 2a with its deviations (the multimodal decisions, "Area 3 decisions", 2026-11-12: the DDPO port, deciding `webp_lossless_bpp` T3 blocking and construct-aligned, `jpeg_q95_kb` T0 supporting, the SigLIP constraint reported only, N 150, K 2, eval seeds 11 and 12 with common random numbers, MM-38 on the deciding metric only, one evaluation run writing `<out>/arm-<metric>/`), MM-17, MM-18, MM-21, MM-24, MM-27, MM-38; design revision 15: 05 §6.1 ("Same programmatic tool", R15-1: a construct-aligned T3 metric is stated in its manifest, the registration and the report), §11 (lineage), §11.2 (derivation `observed`), §12.1 and §12.2 (a `row` record); the area runtime `<mm-root>/area3-sd15/` (README "What gatedtrain needs", "Decisions taken"; `train_ddpo.py` `save_cut`, `base_binding`, `main`; `eval_images.py` `evaluate`; read 2026-11-12) and its CPU sanity outputs.

**Interfaces.** `gatedtrain/ext/adapters/ddpo_sd15.py` (`DdpoSd15Adapter`: the MM-12 interface plus `needs_base`, `consumption_problems`, `training_payloads`, `sampler_problems`); registry rows `ddpo_sd15`, `t2i.sd15_webp_items@0.1` (with `construct_aligned`), `t2i.sd15_jpeg_items@0.1`; `gatedtrain ext-arm-fill ... [--base DIR]`. Generic hooks (used only when an adapter or row declares them): `ext/collect.py` (`training_payloads(..., data=)`, `sampler_problems`, `consumption_fix`, `payload_source`, the `lora_compose` lineage activity, the report's `construct_alignment`), `ext/fill.py` (`--base`, the sampler-hash conversion), `registration.py` (`construct_alignment`), `report.py` (its text line).

#### Requirements

| ID | Source | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| EXT-118 | MM-37, §6 row 2a and deviations | **Registry rows.** Trainer `ddpo_sd15` (`rl.ddpo@0.1`; framework diffusers; pins diffusers, peft, torch, transformers; components the four `deployable` files of `cut.json`; consumption `row`, no fallback; evaluators deciding first; `spec_binding` false). Evaluators `t2i.sd15_webp_items@0.1` (`webp_lossless_bpp`, T3, `construct_aligned` {reward `jpeg_compressibility`, statement}) and `t2i.sd15_jpeg_items@0.1` (`jpeg_q95_kb`, T0): lower, [0, null], not binary, error class `scorer_error`, payload text field `prompt`, item payload `prompt`, `label` (strings; `class_index` is an integer and not a payload), `sampler_keys` the ten keys of `sampler_doc`, samples "150, 2", scorer versions as `eval_images.py` writes them with Pillow 13.0.0, code SHA-256 = `eval_images.py`'s. The SigLIP constraint evaluator has no row (no gate can name it). The docs tables (rows 2a, 2a-s) match. |
| EXT-119 | MM-20 | **Cuts.** `checkpoint-<E>/` is a cut iff `cut.json` parses with `epoch` E, positive integer `global_step` and `samples_consumed`, `deployable` = the four components, `files` listing exactly the five other files with matching SHA-256, the directory holding nothing else, and a whole LoRA safetensors file. `.checkpoint-<E>.partial/` is never a cut. `find_cuts` returns (`global_step`, path): a cut's last step included is its optimizer step. `model_dir_problem` accepts a complete cut or a plain SD1.5 directory (required configs, whole UNet, VAE and text-encoder safetensors). |
| EXT-120 | MM-17, MM-18 | **Identity.** The base alone: `model_hash` = `cut_hash` = its manifest hash. A cut: `adapter_hash` = manifest hash of the four components, `model_hash` = sha256(canonical_json({base, adapter})), `cut_hash` over the cut, `base_ref` {repo, revision, manifest_hash}; `trainer_state.json` is not a component, `sampler.json` is; another base changes `model_hash`; a cut without the base raises. Lineage: the training stage `used` the base (manifest hash = model hash), generates each cut at its global step with its composed model hash, and one `lora_compose` activity per cut `used` the base and the adapter. |

A cut as the spec's base refuses (its hash needs a base). |

Ledger: one record per (sample, applying step) of the epochs up to the last cut, `row`, `observed`, `data_ref` {snapshot = manifest of the spec's prompts, shard, row = sha256(prompt_id)}, `seed_inputs.seed`, `rewards` [reward], `sampler_config_hash`, `sample_id` = SHA-256 of the NUL-joined (run, attempt, epoch, step, prompt_id, seed, member). The impact query on the shard answers `consumed` at `row`. |
| EXT-124 | MM-11 | **Exact overlap** of item `prompt` strings against the shard's prompts and every sampled prompt up to the last cut (`training_payloads(..., data=)`); refused unless allowed, reported either way. |

A `sampler_hash` equal to the compact sorted JSON hash of the arm's own `sampler` (the evaluator's encoding) is converted to the RFC 8785 hash and reported `converted`; any other value refuses. Filled arms collect. |

| EXT-128 | MM-9 to MM-11, MM-16, R15-1 | **Whole run collected:** PASS on the fixture, baseline model hash = the base's manifest hash, candidate = the composed hash, direction lower, contamination 0, precision `row`, 150 items × K 2, GPU seconds = wall seconds = 3600; the report JSON has `construct_alignment` {reward, statement} and the text report a `construct_alignment:` line. |

A GPU request passes; `--spec` is optional and, given, binds the base directory and the prompt directory to read-only mounts. |
| EXT-131 | review 7 M1, MM-10 | **Unhashable base refuses.** When a cut's identity (which composes the base) cannot be computed (a symbolic link in the base, a missing base), ext-collect refuses `EXT-BASE artifacts.<base>.uri` with exit 3, never a traceback (exit 1, read as "gate not passed"). |
| EXT-132 | review 7 M2, MM-24, MM-29 | **Derived log fields re-derived** (`EXT-CONSUMPTION`), as `train_ddpo.py` computes them: `seed` = `derive_seed('sample', run seed, epoch, sample_index)`; `sample_id` = SHA-256 of the compact sorted JSON `["ddpo-sd15", run seed, attempt, epoch, sample_index, prompt_id, seed, member]`; `member` = `sample_index % images_per_prompt`; `reward` = `-jpeg_kb` exactly (a float negation, both written with `repr`, which round-trips: no tolerance); `steps` = [first, last] optimizer step of the sample's epoch. Stdlib only. |
| EXT-133 | review 7 L1, MM-34 | **Evaluation base.** `eval_images.py` loads `--base` unhashed, so a `ddpo_sd15` request or record whose argv runs `eval_images.py` with a `--base` (or `--base=`) other than `<script dir>/models/stable-diffusion-v1-5` refuses (`EXT-LAUNCH --request.argv`, `--recheck.argv`); `train_ddpo.py` is unaffected (its base is bound at collect, EXT-121). |
| EXT-134 | review 7 L3, 05 §6.1 R15-1 | **Statement rules.** `construct_alignment` is refused when the deciding evaluator is not construct-aligned (and, as before, required when it is). The report JSON carries `construct_alignment` {reward, statement (the registration's), manifest_statement (the registry row's)}; the text prints `construct_alignment: <registration's>` and, only when the two differ (whitespace-normalised), `construct_alignment_manifest: <row's>`. Open: the evaluator identity hash does not cover `tier` or `construct_aligned` (unchanged). |
| EXT-135 | review 7 L4 | **One walk of the base.** `identity` of the base reuses the adapter's cached manifest; the cut, the base and the base again walk the base once. |
| EXT-136 | review 7 L5, MM-24 | **Inner epochs.** With `num_inner_epochs` 2 the run collects; each sample gives one record per applying step (2 per sample), sample ids unique; an inner epoch missing a sample refuses. |
| EXT-137 | review 7 L5, EXT-126 | **Conversion is not acceptance.** fill converts the compact hash of an arm's own sampler even when that sampler is not the registered one; collect then refuses the arm (`EXT-ARM`, `sampler` and `sampler_hash`). The conversion works for an `sb3_ppo` arm (a float in its sampler). |
| EXT-130 | MM-38, decision 5 | **Smoke on the deciding metric only.** A full launch judges the smoke on `webp_lossless_bpp` ([0, null]: no mean rule; the mode and sd rules apply); the constraint metric is never judged. |

**Fixtures.** `fixtures/ext/ddpo/`: `items.jsonl` and `train.jsonl` (byte copies of `area3-sd15/items/items.jsonl`, 150 items, and `prompts/train.jsonl`, 248 prompts), `env-freeze.txt` and `adapter_config.json` (from the 2026-11-12 cpu-sanity run and its cut), `sampler.json` (the deployment sampler, 20 steps, 512 px, of the 2026-11-12 base evaluation), `spec.template.yaml` and `registration.template.yaml` (deciding `webp_lossless_bpp`, lower, superiority, margin 0, alpha 0.025, power 0.8, fixture sigma 0.6 and planning effect 0.2, N 150, K 2, seeds 11 and 12, `construct_alignment`, the diffusion sampler). Built by `_ddpo.DdpoScenario`: a runtime stand-in mounted at `/inputs/a3` with a 13-file SD1.5 stand-in base (minimal safetensors), one run of 3 epochs (P 4 × G 2, 2 steps per epoch, cuts at epochs 2 and 3), two `eval_images.py`-shaped evaluation runs with `arm-webp_lossless_bpp/` and `arm-jpeg_q95_kb/` (hard-linked image stand-ins), a GPU launch record (gpu_est_h 1.3), a smoke record and a 3600 s guard log.

**Design choices.** (1) `find_cuts` reports a cut's `global_step` as `last_step_included` (the ledger's `step` is the optimizer step); the directory number is the epoch. (2) Components follow the code (`DEPLOYABLE` includes `base_ref.json`); the README lists three components plus the reference. (3) `sample_id` hashes NUL-joined text, not canonical JSON: the 63-bit noise seed exceeds RFC 8785's safe integers (canonical-JSON rule C4). (4) A sample applied in I inner epochs gives I records (one per applying step); I = 1 in every profile. (5) `rewards` = [the JPEG reward] and `sampler_config_hash` = the RFC 8785 hash of `run_args.json` `sampler` are filled; `gen_version` stays null. (6) `construct_alignment` is a new optional registration field (a naming choice), required for a construct-aligned deciding evaluator; the report prints the registry row's statement. (7) `ext-arm-fill` converts the evaluator's compact-JSON `sampler_hash` (floats such as `1.0` make it differ from RFC 8785) instead of refusing it. (8) The SigLIP constraint arm (`prompt_violation`, extra `eval_meta` keys `constraint_set_hash`, `model_manifest_hash`) is neither registered, filled nor collected in this slice. (9) `item_payload` is `prompt`, `label` (the README proposed `class_index` too, an integer). (10) `spec_binding` false, as `sb3_ppo`: the smoke launch precedes the registration (its sigma comes from the smoke).

**State before implementation (2026-11-12).** 66 tests at the time (`test_base_must_be_a_base_directory` was added later): against the tree before this slice, 63 failed and 3 passed (`test_not_required_for_the_t0_arm` and `test_constraint_evaluator_not_registered`, regression checks, and `test_scorer_code_hash_is_eval_images`, a fixture check).

**Mutants** (a mutation script, scratch copy per mutant, `gatedtrain.__file__` checked, against `test_ext_ddpo.py`): 50, one per new check (adapter, collect hooks, fill, registration, report). 46 killed in the first pass; D12 (epoch sample count), D14 (step of another epoch), D24 (revision), D25 (base files) survived because the tests' message filters also matched a redundant check; the tests now make the log or the base reference otherwise consistent and match the specific message, and all four are killed.

**Review round 8 (review 7 findings M1, M2, L1 to L5; EXT-131 to EXT-137).** 17 tests added first; on the round-7 code 12 failed (M1 exit 1 with a traceback, the five derived fields, the eval `--base` rule and launch, the statement refusal and report, the one-walk count) and 5 passed as regression guards (`test_same_statement_printed_once`, both `TestInnerEpochs`, both `TestConvertedSampler`: the round-7 code already handled these, nothing tested them). Helper change: `_ddpo.simulate` writes one permutation and `updates_per_epoch` steps per inner epoch (`DdpoScenario(inner_epochs=)`). L2 (the request draft's `- '<EPOCHS>'`) is a draft change, no test. Mutants E1 to E13 (one per new check, the same mutation script): all killed.

### 11.21 External-run adapters, slice 4: area "audio" (Whisper base.en SFT on AMI, HF Seq2SeqTrainer) (2026-11-13)

**Moved.** The adapter `hf_seq2seq`, its registry rows and the tests below are in `gatedtrain-recipe-whisper-sft-ami` (section 11.28); file names below are those of the time of writing.

**Sources.** `MULTIMODAL-SPEC.md` section 6 row 5 with its deviations (the multimodal decisions, "Area 4 decisions", 2026-11-13: full-parameter SFT of `openai/whisper-base.en` with transformers `Seq2SeqTrainer` on AMI IHM, deciding per-utterance `wer` T3 and blocking, `sigma_plan: fixed` 0.12, planning effect 0.02, N 500, K 1, eval seed 11, alpha 0.025, power 0.8, full 300 steps, smoke 60, consumption `row` observed, corpus WER descriptive only, R16-1 disclosed), MM-11, MM-17, MM-20, MM-21, MM-23, MM-27, MM-29, MM-33, MM-38; design revision 16: 05 §6.1 ("Shared labelers", R16-1: held-out references made by the training labels' annotation process and consumed by no training stage leave a programmatic scorer at its tier; the registration and the report state the shared convention), §11 (lineage), §11.2, §12.1 and §12.2 (an observed `row` record); the area runtime `<mm-root>/area4-whisper/` (README "Trainer", "Cut layout", "What gatedtrain needs", "Launch request facts"; `train_whisper.py` `save_cut`, `base_binding`, `main`; `eval_asr.py` `evaluate`, `check_cut`; read 2026-11-13; the code is followed where the two differ) and its CPU sanity evidence.

**Interfaces.** `gatedtrain/ext/adapters/hf_seq2seq.py` (`HfSeq2SeqAdapter`: the MM-12 interface plus `consumption_problems`, `sampler_problems`, `argv_problems`, `mount_binding_problems`; no `needs_base`, no `training_payloads`); registry rows `hf_seq2seq` and `asr.whisper_items@0.1` (with `shared_annotation`); `registry.DISCLOSURES`, the one table behind `construct_alignment` (R15-1) and the new `shared_annotation` (R16-1) in `registration.py`, `ext/collect.py` and `report.py`.

**Fixtures.** `fixtures/ext/whisper/`: `env-freeze.txt` (the area venv's 59 distributions, listed with `importlib.metadata` as `write_env_freeze` does), `sampler.json` (the evaluation sampler of the cpu-sanity base arm), `decoding.json` (that sampler without `batch_size`, `dtype`, `order`; byte-identical to a real cut's), `items.sample.jsonl` and `train.sample.jsonl` (three real rows each, shape checks), `spec.template.yaml` and `registration.template.yaml` (deciding `wer`, lower, superiority, margin 0, alpha 0.025, power 0.8, sigma 0.12 fixed, fixture planning effect 0.05 so 46 items suffice, K 1, seed 11, `shared_annotation`, the evaluation sampler). Built by `_whisper.WhisperScenario`: a runtime stand-in mounted at `/inputs/a4` with an 11-file whisper-base.en stand-in base (minimal safetensors), 100 synthetic training utterances and N synthetic items (stand-in WAV bytes), one run of the fixture profile (batch 8: 13 steps per epoch, the 13th carrying 4; 30 steps, cuts 10, 20, 30), two `eval_asr.py`-shaped evaluation runs with `arm-wer/` (hard-linked outputs), a GPU launch record (`area` 5), a smoke record and a 3600 s guard log.

**Design choices.** (1) No consumption fallback (the README proposed `stage`): a log that does not match the run refuses, as for `ddpo_sd15`. (2) `score_outcomes` are `empty_transcript` and `truncated_transcript`, the two scored outcomes `eval_asr.py` marks as sub-metrics (the README proposed `hallucination`, which no code marks). (3) The R16-1 statement is a second registration field, `shared_annotation`, required exactly when the deciding evaluator's row declares it; `construct_alignment` and it share one table (`registry.DISCLOSURES`) and one code path in the registration check, collect and the report. A single `disclosures:` map in the registration was not chosen: it would rename area 3's `construct_alignment`, whose registration is pinned by hash. (4) The disclosure is declared on the evaluator row (static): the row's references are AMI, and the only trainer gating on it trains on AMI. (5) `gradient_accumulation_steps` other than 1 refuses (the runtime uses 1; the per-step rule is stated for one micro-batch). (6) The training `--seed` is not compared with `training_seed` at launch (argv hooks see the argv only); collect compares them. (7) `area` 5 in requests and records (decision scope row 5), the runtime directory being named after the run order. (8) No `eval-smoke-cut` request: the smoke record needs only the baseline, and sigma is fixed.

**State before implementation (2026-11-13).** 96 tests at the time (`TestArgv.test_training_flags` later gained the prefix cases; two tests were added after the mutant run): on the slice-3 code 89 failed and 7 passed, the five `TestRuntimeFacts` that read only the runtime and the fixtures (`test_step_log_shape`, `test_base_files`, `test_scorer_code_hash_is_eval_asr`, `test_fixture_sampler_is_the_evaluators`, `test_fixture_decoding_is_the_cut_file`) and two regression guards (`test_construct_alignment_refused_for_wer`, `test_shared_annotation_refused_where_not_declared`, which the unknown-key rule satisfied before).

**Mutants** (a mutation script, scratch copy per mutant, `gatedtrain.__file__` checked, against `test_ext_whisper.py`): 71, one per new check (adapter, the disclosure loop in registration, collect and report). 68 killed in the first pass; H21 (samples.jsonl epoch), H39 (base_model through the mounts) and H48 (run seed) survived because a redundant check (train_steps.jsonl epoch, model_name_or_path = base_model, settings.seed) matched the test first; the tests now keep the other field consistent (`test_samples_epoch_field`, `test_model_name_or_path_differs` added) and all three are killed.

#### Review round 9 (review 9 findings M1, L1 to L4; EXT-154 to EXT-159; `tests/test_ext_review9.py`, 26)

| ID | Source | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| EXT-154 | review 9 M1 (review 7 M1 class), MM-10 | **Unreadable run and data files refuse** (exit 3, never a traceback, which exits 1 = "gate not passed"), for every adapter: a cut file that cannot be read makes the cut incomplete (`hf_seq2seq`, `ddpo_sd15` `_cut_files_problem`, `sb3_ppo` `model_dir_problem`), so an unreadable last cut refuses `EXT-STAGE` and a middle one is reported in `incomplete_cuts`; an unreadable training shard refuses `SPEC-REF` and the adapters' own data checks return `EXT-DATA` (`_sha_or_none`); an unreadable `env-freeze.txt` refuses `EXT-FREEZE` (collect catches `OSError` around `framework_identity`, every adapter); an unlistable stage directory refuses `EXT-STAGE`; collect also turns an `OSError` from an adapter's `script_problems`, `checks`, `consumption_problems` and `sampler_problems` into a refusal; `stale_outputs` no longer raises on an unsearchable directory; `ext-arm-fill` refuses an unreadable normalizer or environment version. |
| EXT-155 | review 9 L1 | **Arm references** (`EXT-ARM`): an adapter may declare `arm_problems`; for `hf_seq2seq` every `outputs/<item>.json` an arm references names its item and the registered row's `reference_normalized` (an evaluation of another `--items` refuses); an unreadable output refuses. `ddpo_sd15`: `eval_images.py` writes images only (its arm lines and outputs carry no prompt), so there is nothing comparable; the launch rule (EXT-156) binds its items. |
| EXT-156 | review 9 L1, MM-34 | **Evaluation inputs at launch** (`EXT-LAUNCH --request.argv`, `--recheck.argv`; `ddpo_sd15` and `hf_seq2seq`, one helper `runtime_argv_problems`): argv[1] is one of the adapter's scripts in the directory of the pinned interpreter (`<runtime>/.venv/bin/python` → `<runtime>/<script>`; a byte-identical copy elsewhere refuses); the evaluation script's `--base` and `--items` are absent or the defaults (`<runtime>/<base>`, `<runtime>/items`); `--limit` refuses under `--profile full` (the hook now receives the launch or record profile). Options resolve as argparse does; an ambiguous prefix (`--ba`, `--bas`, `--ite`) resolves to nothing, as argparse refuses it. |
| EXT-157 | review 9 L1, 05 §3.4 (G-18) | **Coverage** (no change, pinned): expected items are the registered set × K per arm; two arms that both skip registered items are MISSING (`missing_above_bound`, `max_unpaired` 0), not a smaller gate. |
| EXT-158 | review 9 L2 | **Log beyond the run** (`EXT-CONSUMPTION`): `hf_seq2seq` refuses a `train_steps.jsonl` whose length is not `run_args.json` `max_steps`; `ddpo_sd15` refuses steps or samples of an epoch after `settings.epochs`. |
| EXT-159 | review 9 L3 | **Disclosure table.** Area 3's three `construct_alignment` messages are word for word as before the table (per-entry `missing` and `undeclared` texts); `OPTIONAL_KEYS` is derived from `DISCLOSURES`; `registry.disclosure_problems` checks every declared disclosure is `{<fact>: text, statement: text}` and runs when the registry is imported (before any run directory). |

Tests: `TestUnreadableWhisper` (5), `TestUnreadableDdpo` (4), `TestUnreadableOthers` (3) for EXT-154; `TestArmReferences` (3) EXT-155; `TestEvalArgv` (5) EXT-156; `TestCoverage` EXT-157; `TestLogBeyondTheRun` (2) EXT-158; `TestDisclosureTable` (3) EXT-159. Tests first: on the round-8 code 24 failed and 2 passed (`test_both_arms_limited`, the existing 05 §3.4 behaviour, and `test_optional_keys_follow_the_table`, true of the hand-written tuple). Two test corrections during the fix: `--ite` is ambiguous on `eval_asr.py` (moved to the ambiguous-prefix test), and the sb3 cut test matches the adapter's own message. `test_ext_whisper.TestConsumptionLog.test_cut_beyond_the_log` still sees its `checkpoint-30` message (the `max_steps` rule is reported after the cut checks). Mutants (a mutation script, R1 to R20, one per new check, against `test_ext_review9.py`): 19 killed in the first pass; R3 (sb3 cut guard) survived because collect's generic listing guard also refused; the test now matches the adapter's message, and R3 is killed.

Open: the launcher passes `--gpus all` (fixed in `build_command`, part of the record's label hash); the host has one GPU, so `hf_seq2seq` runs on one device. A per-request device list would change the record schema; not done. A multi-GPU host would make the Trainer use `nn.DataParallel` (32 × n examples per step), which the consumption check refuses after the run.

### 11.22 External-run adapters, slice 5: area "image generation, SD3.5" (Flow-GRPO + LoRA, OCR text rendering) (2026-11-14)

**Moved.** The adapter `flow_grpo_sd35`, its registry rows and the tests below are in `gatedtrain-recipe-flow-grpo-sd35-ocr` (section 11.28); file names below are those of the time of writing.

**Sources.** `MULTIMODAL-SPEC.md` section 6 row 2b with its deviations (the multimodal decisions, "Area 5 decisions", 2026-11-13: the Flow-GRPO port `train_flow_grpo.py`, EMA off, deployment sampler Euler ODE 10 steps CFG 4.5, the base directory untouched and hashed without its `.cache/`, deciding `ocr_b_acc` (docTR) T3 and construct-aligned under R15-1, supporting `ocr_a_acc` (PaddleOCR, the reward), N 400, K 1, eval seed 11, run seed 5000, `filtered` zero-advantage outcomes), MM-11, MM-17, MM-18, MM-20, MM-21, MM-24, MM-27, MM-28, MM-29, MM-33, MM-38; design revision 16: 05 §6.1 (R15-1), §11, §11.2, §12.1 to §12.3 (one outcome enum: `filtered` with reason `zero_advantage`); the area runtime `<mm-root>/area5-sd35/` (README "Outputs of `train_flow_grpo.py`", "Evaluator outputs", "What gatedtrain needs", "Launch request facts"; `train_flow_grpo.py`, `eval_images.py`; read 2026-11-13; the code is followed where the two differ) and its CPU sanity outputs.

**Interfaces.** `gatedtrain/ext/adapters/flow_grpo_sd35.py` (`FlowGrpoSd35Adapter`, a subclass of `DdpoSd15Adapter`: area 3's cut layout, identity, exact overlap and sampler check; its own base directory, base walk without `.cache/`, cut completeness, consumption with the epoch plan, pins, argv rules and run checks); registry rows `flow_grpo_sd35`, `t2i.sd35_ocr_doctr_items@0.1` (with `construct_aligned`), `t2i.sd35_ocr_paddle_items@0.1`. Generic hooks: `canonical.manifest_entries(root, exclude=...)` (top-level names left out; default none); `ext/collect.py` reads an adapter's `base_excludes` (the base manifest leaves them out and the `.cache` refusal does not apply to an excluded `.cache`) and checks the age of `epoch_plan.jsonl` (EXT-64).

**Fixtures.** `fixtures/ext/flow_grpo/`: `items.jsonl` (the first 40 real items, byte slice), `train.jsonl` (the first 64 real shard rows), `env-freeze.txt` and `adapter_config.json` (the 2026-11-13 cpu-sanity run and its cut), `cpu-sanity/` (that run's `run_args.json`, `env-runtime.json`, the three logs, its cut's `cut.json`, `sampler.json`, `base_ref.json`, and the two shard rows it sampled), `eval_meta.ocr_{a,b}_acc.cpu-sanity.json` and `items.ocr_b_acc.cpu-sanity.jsonl` (the arms of the 2026-11-14 CPU evaluation of the cpu-sanity cut, 2 items, with the patched `eval_images.py`), `sampler.json` (the smoke/full deployment sampler: the cpu-sanity cut's with 10 steps, 512 px, fp16 weights, autocast float16 and the eleven sigmas of a 512-px base evaluation; no GPU run exists yet), `spec.template.yaml`, `registration.template.yaml` (deciding `ocr_b_acc`, higher, superiority, margin 0, alpha 0.025, power 0.8, fixture sigma 0.3 fixed and planning effect 0.15, K 1, seed 11, `construct_alignment`). Built by `_flow_grpo.FlowGrpoScenario`: a runtime stand-in at `/inputs/a5` with the 37-file SD3.5 Medium base (stand-in contents, `*.fp16.*` duplicates byte-identical, a `.cache/huggingface/` tree), one run of 3 epochs (P 4 × G 4, 2 steps per epoch; epoch 2 filters a never-rendered group, epoch 3 an always-rendered group and re-includes 2 zero-advantage samples; cuts at epochs 2 and 3), two `eval_images.py`-shaped evaluation runs, a GPU launch record, a smoke record and a 3600 s guard log.

**Design choices.** (1) No consumption fallback (README item 9 proposed `stage`), as areas 3 and 4. (2) A filtered record has `step` null and `sample_id` = H(run, attempt, epoch, `filtered`, prompt_id, seed, member); `rule_version` null. (3) `samples_consumed` is the kept count (once per epoch, whatever `num_inner_epochs`), as `train_flow_grpo.py` counts it; `global_step` and `samples_consumed` 0 are allowed. (4) The "equal rewards ⇒ zero advantage" check runs in one direction only (a group with equal rewards is never plainly kept); the converse (a member at its group mean in a varied group) is not re-derived, since float advantages are numpy's; the zero-advantage padding choice and the prompt draw (numpy/torch generators) are not re-derived either. (5) `item_payload` is `prompt`, `target` (`source_line` is an integer). (6) `construct_aligned.reward` is named `ocr_accuracy_paddleocr`, the supporting arm's scorer name. (7) The request's `area` is 2 (decision row 2b). (8) Target-text overlap always refuses (no `allow_exact_overlap` escape) and is not reported in `contamination` (it is 0 whenever a run is written). (9) `base_excludes` is a generic adapter hook; `manifest_entries` gained an `exclude` argument, used only by this adapter.

**Resolved (maintainers' decisions 2026-11-14).** (1) `eval_images.py` wrote strings (`target`, the OCR texts) and, after a scorer error, nulls into `sub_metrics`, which MM-1 forbids: option (a), the runtime now writes only finite numbers (`ocr_a_acc`, `ocr_b_acc` when present, `nonfinite`; the texts stay in `scores.jsonl`), the shared sampler block unchanged (`sha256:83133e24...`); new `eval_images.py` `sha256:c100a8fb887f93eedbed37b573198cf3bec01508769ebcea7c5ca5a88d8d3b67` (was `c28fd482...`), pinned in the registry rows. A 2-item CPU evaluation of the cpu-sanity cut with it (images byte-identical to the earlier run) passes `ext-arm-fill` against the real base (model hash `sha256:4181b651...`, base `sha256:a19e90d6...`, item set `sha256:0b01553c...`, sampler hash converted) and collect's arm checks (`read_meta`, `meta_differences` against the row, `provenance_problems`, `read_lines`). (2) `lora_compose` ids name the cut directory. (3) Target-text overlap is checked (EXT-176). (4) The other choices above are accepted.

**State before implementation (2026-11-14).** Round 1, 123 tests at the time: with the adapter module present but not registered, 141 errors and failures across them (subtests counted); after wiring, 15 failures: 13 were the fixture arms' string `sub_metrics` (resolved below as decision 1) and 2 were test errors (an edit that tripped an earlier check, a wrong count), fixed in the tests. Round 4 (maintainers' decision on the deciding formula): 3 tests added with the change (the runtime ones need the new scorer; the version test failed on the round-3 registry); a 2-item CPU evaluation of the cpu-sanity cut with `eval_images.py` `fdad8eb9...` regenerated the fixture arms (scorers equal to the registry rows) and passed `ext-arm-fill` against the real base and collect's arm checks. Round 3 (review 10, M1 and L1 to L4): 10 tests added first (with subtests 11 failures on the round-2 code: probes P1 to P5 and P7 as tests, the bool step in all three adapters); after the fix one round-2 test (`test_target_overlap_refused`) was adjusted, since its decoy `target` field is now refused as a mismatch, so it plants the overlap in the prompt. Round 2 (the decisions above): 4 tests added after the code; with both fixes reverted, `test_two_cuts_at_one_global_step`, `test_target_overlap_refused` (both subtests) and `test_target_from_the_prompt` fail; restored, all pass.

### 11.23 External-run adapters, slice 6: area "CAD" (NeMo RL SFT then GRPO with a CadQuery environment; paired executor sidecar) (2026-11-13)

**Moved.** The adapter `nemo_rl_ext`, its registry rows and the tests below are in `gatedtrain-recipe-nemo-rl-cad` (section 11.28); file names below are those of the time of writing.

**Sources.** `MULTIMODAL-SPEC.md` section 6 rows 6 and 6s with the row 6 deviations, MM-34a (the multimodal decisions, "Area 6 decisions", 2026-11-13: trainer NeMo RL in the Tier B image with a custom environment, deciding `iou` T3 with the construct-alignment disclosure, `valid` supporting, `ext-launch` extended for a paired executor sidecar, five families held out of SFT, two gates: SFT on `items-sft` vs the base, GRPO on `items-grpo` vs the SFT cut), MM-11, MM-15, MM-17, MM-20, MM-21, MM-23, MM-27, MM-29, MM-31 to MM-36, MM-38, MM-GAP-12 (resolved); design revision 16: 05 §6.1 (R15-1), §12.1 to §12.3, 06 B18; the area runtime `<mm-root>/area6-cad/` (README "Trainer details", "Outputs", "What gatedtrain needs", "Launch request facts", "Executor sandbox"; `run_cad_nemo.py`, the CAD hooks module, `cad_env.py`, `gen_cad.py`, `score_cad.py`, `cad_protocol.py`, `cad_sandbox.py`, `executor/run-executor.sh`, `executor/BUILD.md`; read 2026-11-13; the code is followed where the two differ) and NeMo RL 4f39fd8 (`grpo_sync.py:1427-1460`, `utils/logger.py:1409-1443`, `utils/checkpoint.py:406`, `utils/checkpoint_storage.py:80-97`, `docker/Dockerfile:227`, `:621`). The NeMo RL code paths of the runtime are untested until the GPU smoke; the fixtures follow the hooks' code as written.

**Interfaces.** `gatedtrain/ext/adapters/nemo_rl_ext.py` (`NemoRlExtAdapter`: both stages, `trainer_ids`, `wants_uses`, `arm_needs_items_dir`, `extra_read_outputs`, `launch_problems`); registry rows `nemo_rl_ext` (with `image_interpreters`, `run_options`, `tmpfs_options`, `sidecar`), `cad.cadquery_items@0.1` (with `construct_aligned`), `cad.cadquery_valid_items@0.1`; registry accessors `image_interpreters`, `sidecar`, `run_options`. Generic hooks: `ext/collect.py` accepts an adapter's `trainer_ids`, passes `uses` to `checks` (`wants_uses`) and `items_dir` to `arm_problems` (`arm_needs_items_dir`), checks the age of `env_calls.jsonl` and of the adapter's `extra_read_outputs` (EXT-64); `ext/launch.py`: `HostConfig.image_interpreters` and `.sidecar`, `bind_argv(image_interpreters=...)`, `sidecar_problems`, `sidecar_commands`, `adapter_launch_problems`, per-script options in `build_command`, `host_config_sha256` with the image interpreters when an adapter has them, the optional request and record key `sidecar`. Areas 1 to 5 are unchanged (no row declares the new keys; their records, hashes and printed commands are identical).

**Fixtures.** `fixtures/ext/cad/executor-uv.lock` (a copy of `area6-cad/executor/uv.lock`, the pinned lock); `fixtures/ext/cad/real-arm/` (the review-11 runtime on the host, CPU: the first 8 generations of `pilot/base-sftgate` executed by `executor/cad_sandbox.py --run-batch` (6 runtime_error, 2 ok) and scored by `score_cad.py --exec` against `items-sft` with `--limit 8`; the scoring output as score_cad.py writes it, `exec_meta.json` copied by score_cad.py itself; 92 KB). Everything else is built by `_nemo_rl_ext.CadScenario` from the runtime's code: a mirrored host (`GATEDTRAIN_EXT_MIRROR_ROOT`) whose container paths are the real ones (the area directory and the Python tree are identity mounts, README "Launch request facts"); a runtime stand-in with the 8-file base, 160 SFT rows of the 10 SFT families and 48 GRPO rows of all 15 in four shards each, 30 items per gate; an SFT run (6 steps of 32, 5 per epoch, cuts 3 and 6) and a GRPO run from SFT cut 6 (4 steps of 16 × 8, 3 per epoch, cuts 2 and 4; rewards with two failures per row, not float32-exact); NeMo RL `step_N` directories with the cut files hard-linked as write_cut does; four scoring runs as the current `score_cad.py` writes them (numeric `sub_metrics` with `status_code` and `truncated`, `framework` {vllm, 0.29.0}, `arm-valid` under `cad.cadquery_valid_items@0.1`, `gen_meta.json` of the evaluated model in the output root); specs and registrations per stage (superiority, `iou`, fixture sigma 0.3, planning effect 0.2, `construct_alignment`), launch records (the GRPO one with its sidecar), smoke records, and a guard log.

**Design choices.** (1) One adapter, two trainer ids (`trainer_ids`); `trainer_id` stays the SFT id for the interfaces that read one. (2) No automatic consumption fallback: a GRPO join that fails at the smoke refuses (the README proposed MM-22 `iteration`; that is a decision after the smoke). (3) The spec's training data is the shard directory `data/<stage>/shards/` (the README names `data/<stage>/` for the snapshot; the shard field and the generic overlap need the shards at the top). (4) NeMo RL's framework `version` is its commit (the image does not show a release version before the smoke). (5) `training_info.json` is required in a cut (NeMo RL writes it, checkpoint.py:406; the hooks copy it when present). (6) The GRPO step's rows are matched as a multiset (the order of the 16 prompts inside a step is not claimed); the join is the rewards in order. (7) The sidecar's limits are type-strict (`cpus` the integer 8, `pids` 256, `core` 0; `True` is not 256); the spool is a separate directory under the output root, not `<run>/spool` (a read-write mount inside the output is refused by MM-34, and the sidecar must not reach the run's outputs). (8) The scoring launch gets the executor's isolation from the registry (`run_options`), not from a request field. (9) `item_payload` has the four string fields (`ref_volume` and `ref_bbox` are not strings).

**State before implementation (2026-11-13).** Tests first: with the registry rows present and the adapter module absent, the module ran 138 tests (subtests counted) with 69 failures and 87 errors; after the adapter and the launch and collect hooks, 3 failures: the fixture guard log dated after the files' mtimes (fixed in the helper) and the docs rows (MULTIMODAL-SPEC row 6, 6s, row 7 wording, fixed). Round 2 (maintainers' decision 2026-11-13: score_cad.py `cffdd9ea...`, composite `d1a95eb5...`): the registry pin, the fixture scorer and the arm checks follow the new format; 14 tests added (status_code rules, gen_meta.json, the real scored arms, the string/null negative test); the replaced `test_failure_scored_above_zero` failed against the new fixture format before it was rewritten. Round 3 (review 11): 12 tests added first; on the round-2 code 18 failures and 4 errors (with subtests, including the full-line command test and the recheck tests broken by the request no longer naming the container); after the fixes all pass except the three `TestRuntimeFacts` tests that read the runtime's `score_cad.py`, `cad_sandbox.py` and `cad_protocol.py`, which were being moved in the runtime at the time. Round 4 (executor redesign, EXT-199): code and tests together; the 13 new tests fail with the exact-mount-set and exec-record checks disabled (mutant run, 16 failures with subtests); all 177 pass, the three runtime-fact tests on the new paths and hashes.

### 11.24 External-run adapters, slice 7: area "VLM" (NeMo RL VLM GRPO of Qwen3.5-0.8B on CLEVR-CoGenT; image interpreters only) (2026-11-14)

**Moved.** The adapter `nemo_rl_vlm`, its registry rows and the tests below are in `gatedtrain-recipe-nemo-rl-vlm-clevr` (section 11.28); file names below are those of the time of writing.

**Interfaces.** `gatedtrain/ext/adapters/nemo_rl_vlm.py` (`NemoRlVlmAdapter`, a sibling of area 6's adapter that imports its NeMo RL helpers: `float32`, the step-log and freeze patterns, `NEMO_RL_COMMIT`; one stage, no sidecar, no executor; `training_payloads`, `payload_source`, `item_overlap_problems`, `arm_needs_items_dir`, `extra_read_outputs`, `mount_binding_problems`, `launch_problems`); registry rows `nemo_rl_vlm` (image interpreters for all three scripts, `run_options` for the two GPU scripts, `interpreter_flags` `-I` for `score_vqa.py`), `vqa.clevr_items@0.1` and `vqa.clevr_valb_items@0.1` (both `construct_aligned` and `shared_annotation`), `SCORE_VQA_SHA256`, `SCORE_VQA_CODE_SHA256`. **Fixtures.** `fixtures/ext/vlm/prompt_clevr_cot.txt` (a copy of the runtime's prompt file); `fixtures/ext/vlm/probe-a6/` (from `out/area7-vlm/probe-a6/train/`: run_args.json, env-freeze.txt, env_calls.jsonl (640 lines), attempts.jsonl, cuts.jsonl byte copies; `nemo/logs/exp_001/train_data_step{1..5}.jsonl` cut down to `rewards` and `idx` per line; `cuts/checkpoint-5/` with its small files copied, the 1.7 GB weights replaced by a minimal safetensors file and the 20 MB tokenizer.json by a text stand-in, and cut.json's `files` re-hashed for those two; `data/shards/shard-00000.jsonl` = the first 80 rows of the real shard 0, the rows of steps 1 .. 5); `fixtures/ext/vlm/real-arm/` (the first 12 items of `out/area7-vlm/gen-base-probe1/score/`, byte copies, and `items-valA-first12.jsonl`; 512 KB in all). Everything else is built by `_nemo_rl_vlm.VlmScenario` from the runtime's code: a mirrored host (`GATEDTRAIN_EXT_MIRROR_ROOT`), the runtime with the real base's 11 file names, 48 training rows in 4 shards, 40 items each in `items-valA` and `items-valB` (their questions repeat the training questions on other images), a full run (60 steps, cuts 20/40/60) and four scoring runs.

**Design choices.** (1) A sibling adapter `nemo_rl_vlm` beside `nemo_rl_ext` (the README allowed an extension or a sibling; MULTIMODAL-SPEC MM-13 named `nemo_rl_ext` for areas 6 and 7 and is updated). (2) No automatic consumption fallback; the join is exact by float32 reward. NeMo RL zeroes the reward of samples that did not end (`vlm_environment.py:229-231`) while the env log keeps the reward `step` returned, so a truncated sample with a non-zero reward would refuse the join; none occurred in the probe (640 of 640), the full run will show whether it does. (3) The env log's 8 samples of a row must be consecutive (observed in the probe; NeMo RL's repeat_interleave), stricter than area 6's count. (4) The overlap key is the image-hash payload; image-only overlap (same image, another question) is not refused (`data_meta.json` reports 0 image overlap by construction). (5) The registered scorer version names Python 3.13.14, the probe-a6 driver's `python` in the image; no in-image scoring has run yet (`gen-base-probe1` was scored on the host's 3.15.2). (6) `vqa.clevr_valb_items@0.1` is T3 with the same disclosures; "supporting only" is the registration's role, not enforced by code (as no other row enforces it). (7) The base's model hash covers all its 11 files (LICENSE included), as area 6. (8) `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` is required for the scoring step too (the generic rule; the scorer loads no model).

**Fixtures.** `fixtures/ext/flow_grpo_wan/probe-train-1/` (from `out/area3-video/probe-train-1/`: run_args.json, env-freeze.txt, env-runtime.json, attempts.jsonl, samples.jsonl, epoch_plan.jsonl, train_steps.jsonl, train_log.jsonl, memory.jsonl byte copies; `checkpoint-2/` with adapter_config.json, base_ref.json, sampler.json, trainer_state.json copied, the 94 MB LoRA replaced by a minimal safetensors file with the real `__metadata__` and two real tensor names, and cut.json's `files` re-hashed for it); `fixtures/ext/flow_grpo_wan/probe-base-1/` (both arms' eval_meta.json and items.jsonl, the 20 `videos/<item>/r0/frames.json`, summary.json, env-runtime.json; the 340 PNG frames left out); `fixtures/ext/flow_grpo_wan/area5-uv.lock` (area 5's lock, 92 KB, the pinned hash); `spec.template.yaml`, `registration.template.yaml`. The items and shard rows are area 5's fixtures (`fixtures/ext/flow_grpo/items.jsonl`, `train.jsonl`), which are byte prefixes of the area 3 copies too. 234 KB in all. `_flow_grpo_wan.WanScenario` builds the rest on a mirrored host (`GATEDTRAIN_EXT_MIRROR_ROOT`; every container path is the real one): the CPython stand-in and area 5's venv link to it, the area 3 runtime with the real base's 21 file names, 64 shard rows and 40 items, a 3-epoch full run with cuts 2 and 3, and two evaluations with 17 frames per video.

**Design choices.** (1) `uses` `rl.flow_grpo_wan@0.1` instead of the decision's `rl.flow_grpo@0.1` (above; the smallest change that keeps `trainer_ids()` a function; area 5's id unchanged). (2) The image and area 5's lock are pinned by the adapter (`launch_problems`), as area 7 pins its images; area 5 itself pins neither. (3) The mount set is exact (the README's launch), so a launch without the Python directory or with an extra mount refuses. (4) Collect copies an arm line's `output_ref` file only, here `frames.json`; the 17 PNG frames stay in the evaluation output (their hashes are in `frames.json`). (5) GPU-h 4.8 replaces the README's 3.3-4.1 (written before `FULL_EPOCHS` went from 40 to 60): smoke 0.21 h measured, full 60 × about 205 s, four evaluations at about 3.9 s per video. (6) `--seed` is required on the training argv (the script defaults to 5000; an implicit seed is refused). (7) The real probe-train-1 run was made with the earlier script (`ad8db67d...`, `FULL_EPOCHS` 40, `save_every` 10); its smoke profile is unchanged, so its logs are valid fixtures, but its launch record would not match today's script hash.

**State before implementation (2026-11-14).** The adapter and the area 5 refactor came first, then the tests; all 103 passed at the first full run after two test fixes (an `ast` reader for constants built from other constants; a value option at the end of argv, which the adapter then refused). Mutation checks on the finished code: with `_reward_field_problems` emptied 13 tests fail, with `_flat_group_problems` reduced to area 5's rule 17, with `launch_problems` emptied 6, with `argv_problems` emptied 20; restored, all pass.

**Attempt 2 (amendment, 2026-11-15; EXT-229 to EXT-232).** Choices: (8) attempt 1's `train_flow_grpo_wan.py` is never edited, because ext-collect re-hashes the launched script against the record (EXT-83) and a changed file would make attempt 1 uncollectable; the amendment's trainer is the copy `train_flow_grpo_wan_v2.py` with only `PROFILES` (`smoke2`, `full2`) and `DEFAULT_PROMPTS` (`prompts-short`) changed, pinned by path like the first. (9) `eval_videos.py` is unchanged (it already takes `--items`), so the evaluator ids, the registry rows and attempt 1's arms stay as they are; the item set an arm was made from is bound through the evaluation's `env-runtime.json` `items_sha256` (new `arm_problems`), since the argv rule no longer fixes one directory. (10) The launch profile stays smoke/full; the v2 script's profile names it (`smoke2`, `full2`), and the Wan adapter hands area 5's profile rule the launched script's profile. (11) `--prompts` is now limited to the script's own directory for both scripts (attempt 1's records never passed it). (12) The GPU-hour ceiling the launcher prints and the smoke budget use 1000, a ceiling high enough not to limit runs (EXT-232), also for the other areas' commands. Tests: the 14 new tests were written with the change; the scenario helper now builds both attempts' data in every runtime and writes the evaluation's `env-runtime.json`; the 103 earlier tests pass unchanged. Mutation checks: `arm_problems` returning nothing fails 2 tests, `_attempt_problems` not called 2, the `--prompts` rule removed 1 (3 subtests); restored, all pass. A real re-collect of attempt 1 (gate/spec.yaml, records/area3-video/train-full-1.launch.json and train-smoke-1.smoke.json, out/area3-video/{full-1, eval-base-1, eval-cand-1}) with this code into a scratch runs root gives the collected result again (FAIL, delta -0.00857, interval [-0.0201, 0.0022], p 0.941), nothing written outside the runs root.

### 11.26 External-run adapters, slice 9: area "VLA" (LeRobot 0.7.0 SmolVLA expert-only SFT on LIBERO-Object, two stages) (2026-11-15)

**Moved.** The adapter `lerobot_smolvla`, its registry rows and the tests below are in `gatedtrain-recipe-lerobot-smolvla-libero` (section 11.28); file names below are those of the time of writing.

**Sources.** `MULTIMODAL-SPEC.md` section 6 row 8 with the row 8 deviations (the multimodal decisions, "Area 8 (VLA) decisions" 2026-11-14, "Area 8 probes P1/P2" and "Area 8 base cut" 2026-11-15: `lerobot/smolvla_base` @ `5e8d12a6...`, SmolVLM2-500M-Video-Instruct @ `4cde9d20...`, `lerobot/libero` @ `a1aaacb7...` episodes 807-1260, expert-only SFT, batch 32, `n_action_steps` 50, a stage-1 ladder of 3,000 steps with cuts every 250 and the base the first cut with probe success in [0.2, 0.6] (checkpoint-500, 0.26), stage 2 from it, deciding binary `success` T3 with both disclosures, 400 gate items × K 2, eval seeds 11 and 12, probe items 40-49 for the base choice and sigma only, per-task success reported), MM-11, MM-17 to MM-21, MM-26, MM-27, MM-29, MM-31 to MM-36, MM-38; design revision 16: 05 §6.1 (R15-1, R16-1), §11, §11.2, §12.1, §12.2; the runtime `<mm-root>/area8-vla/` (README sections 2, 4 to 6 and 11; `train_lerobot_logged.py` sha256 `0bbfba41...`, `a8_common.py` `d01995ca...`, `eval_libero.py` `59cd2182...`; read 2026-11-15) and its GPU outputs (`out/area8-vla/base-1/`: the stage-1 ladder; `base-1-eval2-<step>/`: its probe evaluations with the item timeout).

**Interfaces.** `gatedtrain/ext/adapters/lerobot_smolvla.py` (`LerobotSmolvlaAdapter`: the MM-12 interface plus `normalizer_sha256`, `arm_env_version_sha256`, `consumption_problems`, `sampler_problems`, `arm_problems` with `arm_needs_items_dir`, `argv_problems`, `launch_problems`, `mount_binding_problems`, `extra_read_outputs`, `report_notes`); registry rows `lerobot_smolvla` (`uses` `sft.lerobot@0.1`, `interpreter_flags` `-I` for both scripts) and `libero.lerobot_items@0.1` (with `construct_aligned` and `shared_annotation`), `EVAL_LIBERO_CODE_SHA256`. Generic changes (opt-in, other adapters unchanged): `ext/fill.py` verifies an arm's `env_version_sha256` against `ad.arm_env_version_sha256(arm)` when the adapter declares it (the evaluator writes `env_version.json` into the arm; no cut carries one); `ext/collect.py` appends `ad.report_notes(records_by_arm)` to the report's notes when declared.

**Fixtures.** `fixtures/ext/lerobot/checkpoint-500/` (the small files of `out/area8-vla/base-1/cuts/checkpoint-500/`: config.json, both processor descriptions and their normalizer state files (7.7 KB each), train_config.json, the tokenizer config and chat template, training_state JSON files and the rng state, sampler.json, base_ref.json, and its cut.json as `cut.real.json`; the 1.2 GB weights, the 413 MB optimizer state and the 3.5 MB tokenizer.json are not copied), `base-1/` (run_args.json, env-runtime.json, env-freeze.txt, attempts.jsonl, cuts.jsonl byte copies, the first 3 lines of samples.jsonl), `eval2-500/` (env-runtime.json, summary.json, env_version.json; the arm's eval_meta.json, env_version.json, items.jsonl and one episode log), `items/items-gate.jsonl` and `items-probe.jsonl` (the registered item sets), `area8-uv.lock` (the pinned lock, 152 KB), `spec.template.yaml`, `registration.template.yaml`. 656 KB in all. `_lerobot.LerobotScenario` builds a mirrored host (`GATEDTRAIN_EXT_MIRROR_ROOT`; every container path is the real one): the CPython stand-in and the area venv linking to it, the runtime with stand-in scripts, the real lock, 40 gate and 40 probe items (init states 0-3 and 40-43 of each task), a miniature dataset of the 454 Object episodes with 2 frames each in three parquet files (with one neighbour-suite frame each in the first and last, as the real files 211 and 308) and its frame export, a stage-1 run (40 steps, cuts 20 and 40; checkpoint-20 the base), a stage-2 run (seed 2000, 60 steps, cuts 30 and 60) whose cuts carry the real small files with stand-in weights, two eval_libero.py-shaped evaluations (40 items × K 2 with episode logs), a launch record, a smoke record and a 3600 s guard log.

**Design choices.** (1) The spec's training data is a frame export written by a host step (proposed `export_frames.py`, not yet in the runtime; the runtime writes no JSONL view of the dataset and collect needs `*.jsonl` shards); the adapter re-derives it from the parquet files through the run's mounts, so the export cannot claim frames the run did not read. Shards are named per data file (`chunk-000_file-211.jsonl`), the MM-26 "episode file" reading of LeRobot v3.0. (2) `data_ref.row` = SHA-256 of the RFC 8785 JSON {episode_index, frame_index} (MM-26's "sha256(episode_index, frame_index)" made exact). (3) The exact-overlap key is the item id (`libero_object/t<task>/s<init>`), which no training row carries, so the overlap is 0 by construction; the meaningful check (demonstration initial states vs the benchmark's) needs the original LIBERO HDF5 and stays a disclosed gap. (4) No probe-items evaluator row: `eval_libero.py` writes one evaluator id for both item sets and the item set hash keeps them apart; probe arms are never collected. (5) `NVIDIA_DRIVER_CAPABILITIES` is an allowed name with one allowed value (`compute,utility,graphics`): EXT-92's allow-list replaced EXT-72's `NVIDIA_*` deny-list, and the GPU runs passed it. (6) Stage 1 may be launched with `--profile base` under the launch profile smoke (row 4's operational choice); it is never collected. (7) The decided settings (expert only, batch 32, `n_action_steps` 50) are enforced at launch and collect; another choice is a new decision. (8) The item timeout is pinned at its default 300 s: the registered scorer version names it. (9) Two opt-in generic hooks (fill: the arm's env version; collect: adapter report notes).

**State before implementation (2026-11-15).** The adapter came first, checked by hand against the real base-1 log (95,976 samples: 0 problems), checkpoint-500 (complete; normalizer recomputed) and base-1-eval2-500 (0 arm problems), then the tests; all 103 passed after five test fixes (field prefixes `registration.*`, an `impact_query` shard path, a log line that repeated a frame, a well-formed export edit) and three adapter fixes (a `KeyError` on a line with extra keys, `normalizer_sha256` raising `OSError` for a cut without processor descriptions so collect refuses instead of tracing back, and the export compared on all four columns).

**Mutants** (a mutation script, a scratch copy of the package per mutant, `gatedtrain.__file__` checked, against `test_ext_lerobot.py`): 20, all killed in the first pass: `consumption_problems` empty (14 tests fail), `_base_problems` empty (12), `_data_problems` not called (10), `_run_problems` not called (11), `arm_problems` empty (10), `launch_problems` empty (6), `argv_problems` empty (29), `sampler_problems` empty (3), the cut's normalizer check (1), `_record_problem` none (5), fill without the arm env-version hook (3), `report_notes` unused (1), and one each for the epoch rule, a frame twice in an epoch, the episode log's noise seed, the export's parquet rows, the pinned environment values, the recorded base cut, the rebuilt `lerobot_argv` and a stage-2 cut mounted as the base.

### 11.27 External-run adapters, slice 10: area "agentic RL" (NeMo RL multi-turn GRPO with LoRA, a Python tool in an executor sidecar, the environment as configuration) (2026-11-15)

**Moved.** The adapter `nemo_rl_agentic`, its registry rows and the tests below are in `gatedtrain-recipe-nemo-rl-agentic-math` (section 11.28); file names below are those of the time of writing.

**Sources.** `MULTIMODAL-SPEC.md` section 6 row 10 with the row 10 deviations (the multimodal decisions, "Area 10 (agentic RL) direction", "Area 10 decisions", "Area 10 tool-use probe and choice", 2026-11-15: `Qwen/Qwen3-1.7B` @ `70d244cc...` with LoRA r32, `EleutherAI/hendrycks_math` @ `21a56338...` integer-answer subset, 2,000 training rows, 500 test items, generated code only in a separate sidecar, exact match deciding with T3 and both disclosures, N 500, K 1); the area runtime `<mm-root>/area10-agentic` (README section 0, in particular 0.4 to 0.11; `run_agentic_nemo.py` 04603e83, the agentic hooks module, `math_code_env.py`, `agentic_core.py` c1076f0a, `traj_log.py`, `consumption.py`, `executor/` (exec_protocol.py cbd8df69, executor_server.py 42971d92, sandbox_runner.py 6e1082ec), `gen_agentic.py` 8f32dcd2, `score_agentic.py` a3bc32c8; read 2026-11-15); its GPU probe `out/area10-agentic/p1-a3/train` (3 steps at the final settings; cut into `fixtures/ext/agentic/p1-a3/`); NeMo RL 4f39fd8 (rollouts.py:1031-1278, grpo.py:4667-4691, logger.py:1427-1441, sliding_puzzle.py:265-342); design revision 16, 05 section 6.1 (R15-1, R16-1), section 12.2.

**Interfaces.** `gatedtrain/ext/adapters/nemo_rl_agentic.py` (`NemoRlAgenticAdapter`: the MM-12 interface plus `needs_base`, `consumption_problems`, `extra_read_outputs`, `training_payloads`, `item_overlap_problems`, `arm_problems` with `arm_needs_items_dir`, `sampler_problems`, `report_notes`, `argv_problems`, `launch_problems`, `mount_binding_problems`, and `wants_evaluator`; the environment table `ENVS`); registry rows `TRAINER_ROWS["nemo_rl_agentic"]` (with `envs` and a sidecar with `image_interpreter`) and `EVALUATOR_ROWS["agentic.math_code_items@0.1"]` (with `env_id`); two opt-in generic hooks: `launch.sidecar_problems` and the record writer accept a sidecar row's `image_interpreter` (EXT-249), `collect` passes the gate's evaluator to `checks` of an adapter that declares `wants_evaluator` (EXT-253). Every other adapter is unchanged.
